- **built-with**: Passive, headless recon of a web app's frontend to infer its backend and tech stack - API endpoints, third-party vendors, CSP allow-lists, auth provider, framework/build tooling, and public config tokens - then produce a plain-English vendor dossier (Artifact + landscape PDF).
